####################################################################################################
ORIGINAL PATH: MASTER_DATA_CONSTITUTION.md
####################################################################################################

# iSEPADU AI: Master Data Constitution
## Constitutional Foundation for the School AI Operating System

## 1. Executive Summary & Core Philosophy

The **iSEPADU AI Master Data Constitution** serves as the supreme governance standard for all data architectures, relationship models, system parameters, and access patterns within the **iSEPADU AI** platform.

To transform iSEPADU AI from a single-school application into a universal **AI Operating System for Schools**, the platform must operate under a new, absolute architectural mandate:

> **Configure, Don’t Code.**

No school structure, committee, role, subject, or class rule should be hardcoded. Instead, the platform is driven by a configurable, metadata-driven engine. This document establishes the constitutional 8-layer architecture, laying down the structural foundation that ensures data integrity, single source of truth (SSOT) compliance, and seamless cross-workspace integration across all school portfolios.

---

## 2. The 8-Layer Constitutional Architecture

Every data transaction, user operation, administrative review, and AI recommendation in iSEPADU AI inherits its context from this strict structural hierarchy:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│             LAYER 0: SCHOOL CONFIGURATION              │
│  (Type, Category, Enabled Subjects, Governance Rules)  │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Instantiates
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│          LAYER 1: MASTER DATA CONSTITUTION             │
│   (Immutable SSOT Student, Personnel, Class, Subject)  │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Overlays
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           LAYER 2: SCHOOL GOVERNANCE ENGINE            │
│  (MySG Committees, Portfolios, Authority RACI Matrix)  │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Allocates
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│        LAYER 3: TEACHING RESOURCE ALLOCATION           │
│   (Roster Assignments, Teaching Roles, Weekly Periods) │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Governs
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           LAYER 4: PROJECT LIFECYCLE ENGINE            │
│  (iSEF PDCA State Machine, Evidence, Review Hierarchy) │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Feeds
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              LAYER 5: WORKSPACE MODULES                │
│    (Academic, HEM Welfare, Kokurikulum, Admin, HR)     │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Aggregates
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           LAYER 6: DASHBOARD & ANALYTICS               │
│    (School Health Index, PBPPP Mapping, SKPMg2 Logs)   │
└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                            │ Supports
                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                LAYER 7: AI ASSISTANT                   │
│   (Stateless Decision Co-pilot, Cognitive Guardrails)   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 2.5 The Connected School Operating Model: Unified Operational Pipeline

To prevent disparate data stores and ensure seamless coordination across iSEPADU AI, all subsystems must consume a unified **School Operating Model**. This connected pipeline transitions organizational and operational entities through a linear chain of custody:

```
Personnel Node
     │
     ▼ (Assigned To)
Teaching Allocation ──┐
     │                │
     ▼ (Grouped Into) ▼ (Linked By Workload)
Governance Assignment ──► Committee Structure
                            │
                            ▼ (Launches & Plans)
                          Project Assignment (iSEF)
                            │
                            ▼ (Evaluated & Mentored by)
                          Monitoring Responsibility
                            │
                            ▼ (Yields Key Metrics for)
                          Performance Evaluation (PBPPP / SKPMg2)
                            │
                            ▼ (Aggregates Into)
                          Analytics (School Health Index)
```

The execution flow of this operating model operates as follows:

1.  **Personnel Node:** The root of all human capital in the school. Every single individual—from the executive leadership and teaching staff to support and contract staff—is declared as a master Personnel record.
2.  **Teaching Allocation:** Personnel are mapped to classroom instruction blocks, formulating the granular, sessional mapping of who teaches which class, which subject, for how many periods, under what role, and when (timetable slot).
3.  **Governance Assignment:** Concurrently, personnel are mapped to administrative, policy, and tactical assignments (Portfolios, Committees, Coordinators, Panels, Working Groups, Task Forces, and Temporary Committees) establishing formal RACI roles.
4.  **Committee Structure:** Governance assignments are aggregated into functional committees, standing boards, and panels, designating clear leadership hierarchies (e.g., Pengerusi, Setiausaha, AJK) to govern specific school domains.
5.  **Project Assignment (iSEF):** Committees or designated personnel launch structured, polyphasic PDCA projects (e.g., academic interventions, student welfare campaigns, co-curricular initiatives) and assign specific task action items to Personnel PICs.
6.  **Monitoring Responsibility:** Assigned managers and administrators (e.g., Ketua Panitia, PK, Guru Besar) supervise the execution of actions. They audit submitted evidence (narratives, files, URLs, or metrics) and provide coaching comments or revision directives.
7.  **Performance Evaluation:** Upon project completion, the system analyzes the PIC's performance metrics (compliance, completion rate, target achievements) and translates them directly into annual professional evaluations (PBPPP) and institutional self-audits (SKPMg2).
8.  **Analytics:** All performance indexes, PBD score distributions, and committee operational statistics feed into the master **School Health Index**, providing real-time strategic intelligence.

---

## 3. Layer 0 – School Configuration Layer

The foundational bootstrap layer of the operating system. Before any workspace or master record can exist, a school must establish its profile metadata. Modifying these configurations updates the operational constraints of the system in real-time.

### 3.1 School Profile & Structural Attributes
*   **School Profile Registry:**
    *   `school_code`: National registration identifier (e.g., JBCxxxx).
    *   `official_name`: Full legal name of the school (e.g., SJK(C) Ek Hua).
    *   `school_type`: `SK` (National) | `SJKC` (Chinese Vernacular) | `SJKT` (Tamil Vernacular). Maps default language settings and standard cultural curriculum requirements.
    *   `school_category`: `SKM_A` (Under-enrolled Grade A) | `SKM_B` (Under-enrolled Grade B) | `GRED_A` (Ordinary Grade A) | `GRED_B` (Ordinary Grade B). Enforces specific administrative staffing structures and budget baselines.
    *   `has_preschool`: Boolean. Toggles preschool classroom masters and specialized early childhood curriculum modules.
    *   `has_ppki`: Boolean (Special Education Integration Program). Toggles accessibility controls, diagnostic portfolios, and specialized teacher resources.

### 3.2 Sessional & Operational Parameters
*   **Temporal Configuration:**
    *   `academic_session_structure`: Defines session segments (e.g., 2-semester, 3-term, or custom sessional partitions).
    *   `weekly_teaching_days`: Configurable schedule mask (e.g., Monday-Friday vs. Sunday-Thursday for Johor/Kelantan/Terengganu) to calibrate default operational timelines.
    *   `daily_periods_limit`: Maximum teaching periods per day to prevent scheduling overflows.

### 3.3 Dynamic Engine Templating
*   **Curricular & Subject Configuration:**
    *   `enabled_subjects_registry`: A dynamic checklist of subjects active in the current school. Schools can toggle subjects (e.g., Mandarin, Jawi, Bahasa Arab) on/off without editing core code.
*   **Governance & Committee Templates:**
    *   Allows administrators to activate or deactivate MySG (Malaysia School Governance) templates or create school-specific standing committees, custom portfolios (e.g., Penyelaras iSEF), and coordinator types (e.g., Penyelaras RMT, Penyelaras SPBT) purely through configuration.
*   **Class Naming & Progression Rules:**
    *   `class_naming_schema`: Standardized nomenclature rules (e.g., Numeric-Virtue like "4 Cerdik", Color-based, or Letter-based) automatically applied during seasonal sessional transitions.

---

## 4. Layer 1 – Master Data Constitution

Formed dynamically upon instantiation from Layer 0. This layer represents the absolute Single Source of Truth (SSOT) directory, protecting core data nodes from sessional drift.

### 4.1 Master Schemas

#### A. School Master
Contains institutional metadata derived from Layer 0, establishing the parent namespace for all downstream collections.

#### B. Academic Year Master
*   Defines active and historical cycles (e.g., "2026/2027").
*   Only *one* record is marked `active: true` for writing. All others are marked `archived: true` (immutable, read-only).

#### C. Personnel Master (Comprehensive Staff Registry)
*   **Inclusive Scope:** Not limited to teachers. Holds details of ALL school personnel to drive school-wide authentication and assignments:
    1.  **Executive Leadership (Pentadbir):** Guru Besar (Principal), Penolong Kanan (PK) Pentadbiran, PK HEM, PK Kokurikulum, PK PPKI (Special Education), PK Petang (Afternoon Session).
    2.  **Academic Staff / Educators (Guru):** Guru Akademik Biasa, Guru Perpustakaan & Media (GPM), Guru Data, Guru Bimbingan & Kaunseling (GBK), Guru Pemulihan Khas.
    3.  **Anggota Kumpulan Pelaksana (AKP - Administrative & Support Staff):** Ketua Pembantu Tadbir (KPT), Pembantu Tadbir (PT), Pembantu Operasi (PO), Pembantu Pengurusan Murid (PPM - for Preschool and PPKI), Juruteknik Komputer (IT Tech), Pembantu Makmal (Lab Assistant).
    4.  **Authorized External / Support Staff:** Contract educators, security supervisors, or cleaning staff liaisons.
*   **Operational Properties:** Tracks Civil Service details (MyKad, grade of service e.g., DG44/N19/FT29, employment status e.g., Active/On-Leave/Transferred).
*   **Security & Auth:** Acts as the unique system authentication node.

```typescript
interface PersonnelMaster {
  personnel_id: string; // Unique primary key (MyKad/National ID)
  official_name: string;
  email: string;
  phone_number: string;
  personnel_type: 'EXECUTIVE' | 'EDUCATOR' | 'AKP' | 'EXTERNAL_SUPPORT';
  service_grade: string; // e.g., "DG44", "DG41", "N19", "FT29", "C19"
  designation: string; // e.g., "Guru Besar", "PK HEM", "Guru Penyelaras ICT", "Pembantu Tadbir"
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'ON_LEAVE' | 'TRANSFERRED' | 'RETIRED';
  has_ppki_access: boolean; // PPKI workspace toggle
  has_preschool_access: boolean; // Preschool workspace toggle
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
```

#### D. Student Profile Master (Demographics)
*   The immutable master register of all pupils. Holds demographic information that does not drift when a student transitions classes.
*   **Demographics Registry:** Contains Name, MyKid/Passport, Date of Birth, Gender, Race, Religion, and Citizenship.
*   **System Isolation:** Isolated from academic year progression. Students progress through sessional Enrollment records, leaving the baseline profile untouched.
*   **Financial and Welfare Isolation:** In accordance with strict privacy and data progression frameworks, household income, financial status, and assistance eligibility (e.g., eligibility for RMT, SPBT, or BAP) are excluded from the permanent Student Profile. Instead, they are maintained as isolated, session-based welfare records that can be calibrated and audited dynamically each academic year.

```typescript
interface StudentProfile {
  student_id: string; // Unique permanent ID (MyKid or National Student ID - APDM ID)
  official_name: string;
  gender: 'L' | 'P';
  dob: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  race: 'Melayu' | 'Cina' | 'India' | 'Bumiputera_Sabah' | 'Bumiputera_Sarawak' | 'Lain-lain';
  religion: string;
  citizen_status: 'Warganegara' | 'Bukan_Warganegara' | 'Penduduk_Tetap';
  guardian_contact: {
    name: string;
    relationship: string;
    phone: string;
    email?: string;
  };
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
```

#### E. Student Enrollment Master (Sessional Mapping)
*   Separated from the baseline profile. Standardizes how students are assigned to years and classes.
*   **Core Parameters:** Maps Student Profile ID to Academic Year ID, Class ID, and Enrollment Status.

```typescript
interface StudentEnrollment {
  enrollment_id: string; // Compound Key: [student_id]_[academic_year_id]
  student_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to StudentProfile
  academic_year_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to Academic Year Master
  class_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to Class Master
  enrollment_status: 'ACTIVE' | 'TRANSFERRED' | 'SUSPENDED' | 'GRADUATED' | 'WITHDRAWN';
  status_change_date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  status_change_reason?: string; // e.g., "Pindah Sekolah", "Gantung Sekolah"
  remarks?: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
```

#### F. Class Master
*   Unique compound key: `academic_year_id` + `standard_level` + `class_name`.
*   Stores class limits, physical classroom parameters, and references the primary Class Teacher.

#### G. Subject Master
*   Directly populated by the enabled subjects registry in Layer 0.
*   Maps national standard codes, default languages, and instructional periods.

---

## 5. Layer 2 – School Governance Engine

The structural mapping that translates physical school personnel into active roles of authority, accountability, and execution.

### 5.1 Dynamic Governance Assignment
A user’s physical personnel record (Layer 1) is decoupled from their active sessional governance assignment through a constitutional overlay mechanism. Governance Assignment remains a strict constitutional overlay of the Personnel Master, avoiding unnecessary structural expansion into additional ownership models unless absolutely required. Governance assignments are dynamic, sessional, and structured to support all standard MySG and customized school organizational groupings.

#### A. Governance Assignment Node Types:
1.  **Portfolio (Sektor Pentadbiran):** Permanent administrative sectors governed by Penolong Kanan leaders (e.g., Kurikulum, Hal Ehwal Murid, Kokurikulum, PPKI, Petang).
2.  **Committee (Jawatankuasa):** Formally constituted policy, action, or steering boards (e.g., JKPPS, JKKS, JKHEM, JKKOKO, JK Kewangan, JK Aset).
3.  **Coordinator (Penyelaras):** Focused administrative coordinators assigned to execute specific regulatory mandates (e.g., Penyelaras iSEF, Penyelaras SPBT, Penyelaras RMT, Penyelaras Jadual Waktu).
4.  **Panel (Panitia Mata Pelajaran):** Subject-level academic groupings (e.g., Panitia Bahasa Melayu, Panitia Matematik).
5.  **Working Group (Kumpulan Kerja):** Execution-oriented task groups for localized workflows (e.g., Kumpulan Kerja Kebersihan Tandas, Kumpulan Kerja Pendaftaran).
6.  **Task Force (Pasukan Khas):** High-urgency, rapid-response clusters handling targeted school emergencies or system overhauls (e.g., Pasukan Khas Kebakaran, iSEPADU Tech Task Force).
7.  **Temporary Committee (Jawatankuasa Pelaksana Acara):** Time-bounded, event-specific boards active only for the duration of a school calendar event (e.g., JK Pelaksana Hari Sukan Negara, JK Kejohanan Merentas Desa).

```typescript
interface GovernanceAssignment {
  assignment_id: string; // Unique primary key
  personnel_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to PersonnelMaster
  academic_year_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to Academic Year Master
  governance_type: 'PORTFOLIO' | 'COMMITTEE' | 'COORDINATOR' | 'PANEL' | 'WORKING_GROUP' | 'TASK_FORCE' | 'TEMPORARY_COMMITTEE';
  entity_name: string; // Name of the structural grouping (e.g., "Panitia Matematik", "JKKS")
  role: 'CHAIR' | 'VICE_CHAIR' | 'SECRETARY' | 'COMMITTEE_MEMBER' | 'COORDINATOR_LEAD' | 'OPERATIONAL_STAFF';
  raci_role: 'R' | 'A' | 'C' | 'I'; // Overlaid directly onto system decision endpoints
  effective_date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  expiry_date?: string; // For temporary committees or task forces
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'INACTIVE' | 'DELEGATED';
}
```

### 5.2 Standing Committees (Jawatankuasa)
*   **Dynamic Committee Instantiation:** Standing bodies are constructed by grouping active `GovernanceAssignment` records under a parent committee ID.
*   **Default MySG Committees:**
    *   *JK Pengurusan & Pentadbiran Sekolah (JKPPS)*
    *   *JK Kurikulum Sekolah (JKKS)*
    *   *JK Hal Ehwal Murid (JKHEM)*
    *   *JK Kokurikulum Sekolah (JKKOKO)*

### 5.3 Authority RACI Matrix & Accountability Chains
*   Every critical system action (approving budget, locking PBD scores, recording suspensions, launching projects) validates against the assigned `raci_role` in the active `GovernanceAssignment` registry.
*   **System Action Permissions:** Rather than hardcoding authorization, the platform queries the RACI matrix to verify that the Personnel ID executes within a valid authorization window.

---

## 6. Layer 3 – Teaching Resource Allocation Layer

Rather than simple teacher-to-subject lists, Layer 3 is the core optimization engine managing the school's teaching workloads, pedagogical coverage, and schedules. It serves as the data bridge between standard educational inputs and operational execution.

### 6.1 Future-Ready Allocation Schema
Every teaching assignment is structured to follow a strict connection chain:
`Teacher → Academic Year → Subject → Class → Weekly Period → Teaching Role → Timetable Slot`

```typescript
interface TeachingAllocation {
  allocation_id: string; // Unique primary key
  teacher_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to PersonnelMaster (Teacher)
  academic_year_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to Academic Year Master
  subject_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to Subject Master
  class_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to Class Master
  weekly_periods: number; // Target periods/hours allocated per week
  teaching_role: 'MAIN' | 'ASSISTANT' | 'RELIEF' | 'OBSERVER';
  
  // Future-ready Timetable Slot alignment mapping
  // This is strictly OPTIONAL and is generated and maintained entirely by the future Timetable Engine, not the Master Data layer.
  timetable_slots?: {
    day: 'MONDAY' | 'TUESDAY' | 'WEDNESDAY' | 'THURSDAY' | 'FRIDAY' | 'SATURDAY' | 'SUNDAY';
    period_number: number; // 1-indexed period slot
    start_time: string; // HH:MM (24-hour format)
    end_time: string; // HH:MM (24-hour format)
    room_id?: string; // Location parameter (e.g., "Kelas 4 Cerdik", "Makmal Sains")
  }[];
}
```

### 6.2 Workload Threshold and Allocation Constraints
*   **Weekly Workload Threshold Rules:**
    *   **Recommended Threshold:** 32 periods per week.
    *   **Warning Threshold:** 33+ periods per week. The system displays a warning message to prevent educator burnout.
    *   **Administrator Override:** Fully allowed. This accommodates the operational reality of SKM (Sekolah Kurang Murid) and smaller schools where teachers may legitimately teach more than 32 periods.
    *   **Audit Compulsion:** Every administrator override of the weekly teaching period threshold must be automatically recorded with a justification entry in the centralized system audit log.

### 6.3 Relational Downstream Consumers

```
                             ┌─────────────────────────────────┐
                             │  LAYER 3: TEACHING ALLOCATION   │
                             └────────────────┬────────────────┘
                                              │ Shared Context
       ┌───────────────────────┬──────────────┼──────────────┬───────────────────────┐
       ▼                       ▼              ▼              ▼                       ▼
┌──────────────┐        ┌──────────────┐┌──────────────┐┌──────────────┐       ┌──────────────┐
│ ASSESSMENT & │        │  TIMETABLE   ││ TEACHER WORK ││ RELIEF MGMT  │       │ INSTITUTIONAL│
│  PBD MARKS   │        │  GENERATION  ││ LOAD METRICS ││ (GURU GANTI) │       │ EVALUATIONS  │
│  Validates   │        │   Informs    ││  Calculates  ││  Determines  │       │ (PBPPP/SKPM) │
│ input rights │        │ period grids ││ duty balance ││ availability │       │ Feeds logs   │
└──────────────┘        └──────────────┘└──────────────┘└──────────────┘       └──────────────┘
```

1.  **Assessment & PBD Marks Input:** Validates write permissions. Only teachers assigned as `MAIN` or `ASSISTANT` on a classroom block can record, edit, or submit continuous assessment (PBD) scores and exam grades.
2.  **Timetable Generation:** Provides the basic relational nodes (Teacher + Class + Subject + Periods) required to generate non-conflicting master schedule grids.
3.  **Teacher Workload Analytics:** Automatically aggregates allocated weekly periods across all assigned main and assistant blocks to compute real-time workload statistics (e.g., weekly contact hours), optimizing teacher utilization.
4.  **Relief Management (Guru Ganti):** Analyzes active allocations to determine teacher availability during a given period and automatically suggest non-conflicting relief teachers.
5.  **Institutional Evaluation (PBPPP & SKPMg2):** Feeds active teaching allocations and subject mastery metrics directly into standard performance appraisal (PBPPP) and quality audit (SKPMg2 Standard 4) modules.
6.  **AI Strategic Planning:** Provides the core data matrix for the AI Decision Engine to predict educational outcomes, balance curriculum coverage, and suggest tailored interventions.

---

## 7. Layer 4 – Project Lifecycle Engine (iSEF Engine 01)

The state-machine engine governing the operational execution of school improvement initiatives across all workspaces.

### 7.1 PDCA Workflow Integration
Inherits governance roles and resource allocations from Layers 0-3 to transition active school projects through the structured PDCA steps:
1.  **Plan:** Draft proposals configured with milestones, due dates, and PICs.
2.  **Approve:** Validation against the Layer 2 Authority Matrix. Projects transition to "Approved" only when authorized by the designated Portfolio Executive (e.g., PK Pentadbiran for Academic, PK HEM for Student Affairs).
3.  **Execute:** Execution steps assigned to PICs.
4.  **Monitor:** Review and coaching workflows managed by Middle Management and Executives.
5.  **Evaluate / Act:** Baseline-to-target calculations to evaluate impact, and formal archiving of key compliance findings.

### 7.2 Core Capabilities
*   **Structured Evidence Engine:** Supports qualitative narrative reflection, quantitative verification metrics, external cloud vault references (Google Drive/Sheets), and local metadata placeholders.
*   **Reminders, Escalation & Overdue Rules:** Multi-tier automated reminder rules (7 days before, 3 days before, due today) and formal departmental escalations (Level 1 to Ketua Panitia, Level 2 to Guru Besar) when action items remain outstanding.

---

## 8. Downstream Execution Layers

### 8.1 Layer 5 – Workspace Modules
Polymorphic, vertical modules representing the physical branches of school administration. Each workspace consumes the same underlying constitutional layers:
*   **Academic (Kurikulum) Workspace:** Leverages teaching resource allocations and class lists to manage PBD diagnostics, exam analysis, and subject-level academic interventions.
*   **HEM (Student Affairs) Workspace:** Manages student welfare, attendance trackings, behavioral disciplinary logs, and nutritional/textbook loan support.
*   **Kokurikulum Workspace:** Governs sports rosters, co-curricular point calculations (PAJSK), and athletic event achievements.
*   **Administration & HR Workspace:** Manages asset tracking, school operational calendars, teacher appraisals, and staff training records.

### 8.2 Layer 6 – Dashboard & Analytics
The school-wide executive summary layer. It aggregates transaction logs, lifecycle completion metrics, and performance indicators into standard quality indices:
*   **SKPMg2 & PBPPP Reporting:** Translates active project outcomes and teaching performance into official national compliance scores.
*   **School Health Index:** A unified dashboard reflecting the overall academic, social, physical, and administrative performance of the school.

### 8.3 Layer 7 – AI Assistant
The cognitive co-pilot of the school.
*   **Decision Support Mandate:** The AI acts strictly as a **decision support engine**, generating draft proposals, identifying curriculum gaps, and suggesting intervention strategies.
*   **Zero Executive Action:** The AI cannot modify active, running projects, alter master records, or bypass the human-in-the-loop review screens. All proposals must be actively accepted, modified, or rejected by authorized human educators.
*   **Stateless Operations:** The AI does not store sensitive personal student records locally. It queries the Master Data layer on-demand using secured tokens.

---

## 9. Data Integrity & Sync Policies

### 9.1 Master Data Protection Rule (Read-Mostly Constitutional Policy)
*   **The Read-Mostly Directive:** 
    "Master Data is Read-Mostly.
    Only System Administration modules may modify constitutional data.
    All engines consume Master Data.
    No engine may redefine, duplicate, or own constitutional data."
*   **Separation of Concerns:** Under no circumstances shall an external engine or temporary workspace (such as HEM, Kokurikulum, or iSEF) replicate or override core student or staff lists. They must read directly from the constitutional Layer 1 records.

### 9.2 Cross-Workspace Separation of Concerns
*   **No Redundant Roster Syncing:** Under no circumstances shall a workspace (such as HEM or Kokurikulum) replicate student or staff listings. All workspaces read dynamically from Layer 1.
*   **Write Isolation:** Workspaces can write to their portfolio-specific collections (e.g., discipline infractions, sport tournament medals), but any alteration to core master fields (e.g., Class lists, Personnel grades) must go through the Layer 0/1 Admin Configuration panel.

### 9.3 Transactional Audit Logs
Every write, deletion, role assignment, or state change across the 8 layers must trigger an entry in the centralized `audit_trail` directory:
```typescript
interface AuditTrailEntry {
  timestamp: string;
  actor_id: string;
  actor_name: string;
  actor_role: string;
  action_type: 'CREATE' | 'UPDATE' | 'DELETE' | 'STATE_CHANGE' | 'AUTHORIZE' | 'IMPORT' | 'ROLLBACK';
  layer: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6 | 7;
  entity_id: string;
  details: string; // Dynamic narrative describing changed values
}
```

### 9.4 Intelligent Incremental Import System (IDS)
To manage bulk data ingestion while ensuring absolute Single Source of Truth (SSOT) integrity, iSEPADU AI mandates an Intelligent Incremental Import System (IDS). All data ingestion operations (such as CSV/JSON uploads of students, staff, and classes) must flow through this four-part standard:

#### A. Import Session
Every ingestion pipeline executes within a unique, isolated, audit-ready session block:
```typescript
interface ImportSession {
  session_id: string; // Unique UUID
  timestamp: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
  imported_by_uid: string; // Foreign Key pointing to PersonnelMaster
  imported_by_name: string;
  import_type: 'PERSONNEL' | 'STUDENT_MASTER' | 'STUDENT_ENROLLMENT' | 'CLASS_ALLOCATION' | 'SUBJECT_ALL';
  records_count: {
    total_received: number;
    inserted: number;
    updated_fills: number;
    conflicted_skipped: number;
    skipped_identical: number;
  };
  raw_payload_checksum: string; // SHA-256 hash of the uploaded CSV/JSON payload
}
```

#### B. Conflict Resolution Engine
Rather than blindly overwriting data, IDS matches incoming records against active DB records using unique natural identifiers (MyKad, MyKid, or APDM ID) and processes conflicts based on strict state transition rules:

| Condition | Diagnostic Status | Ingestion Outcome | Log Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Identical Records** | Incoming row matches active DB properties exactly. | **Skip** record. No database write. | Log as `SKIPPED_IDENTICAL`. |
| **Field-level Null Backfill** | Active DB is missing properties (null/empty) that are supplied in the incoming row. | **Merge & Update** empty fields. Preserve existing fields. | Log as `MERGED` with changed keys. |
| **Active Conflict** | Active DB has a non-null property that differs from the incoming row. | **Flag Conflict (Default)**. Halt row writing to prevent corruption, or **Force Overwrite** if admin explicitly overrides. | Log as `CONFLICT_FLAGGED` or `OVERWRITTEN_FORCE`. |

#### C. Rollback History (Transactional Reversibility)
To safeguard against catastrophic administrative upload errors, every Import Session must preserve state snapshots enabling complete, single-click transactional rollback.
*   **Availability Guidelines:** Rollback is session-based. Rollback remains fully available and active until the import session is explicitly archived by the administrator.
*   **Authorization:** Rollbacks require explicit administrative approval (as a custom override check) and are tracked cell-by-cell in the IDSAuditTrail.

```typescript
interface RollbackHistory {
  rollback_id: string; // Unique UUID
  session_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to ImportSession
  timestamp: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
  rolled_back_by_uid: string; // Foreign Key pointing to PersonnelMaster
  status: 'PENDING' | 'ROLLED_BACK' | 'FAILED';
  reverted_records: {
    collection: string; // e.g., "personnel", "student_master"
    entity_id: string; // Key of changed record (e.g., MyKad number)
    previous_state: Record<string, any> | null; // Null if record was a new insert
    imported_state: Record<string, any>; // Snapshot of what was imported
  }[];
}
```

#### D. IDS Audit Trail
An immutable, linear log tracking cell-by-cell ingestion actions. This registry is distinct from standard system audit trails to enable rapid diagnostic parsing.
```typescript
interface IDSAuditTrail {
  ids_audit_id: string; // Unique UUID
  session_id: string; // Foreign Key pointing to ImportSession
  row_index: number; // Row index in original file payload
  natural_key: string; // Unique reference identifier (e.g., IC, APDM ID)
  action: 'INSERTED' | 'MERGED' | 'OVERWRITTEN' | 'SKIPPED_CONFLICT' | 'SKIPPED_IDENTICAL';
  fields_changed?: {
    field_name: string;
    old_value: any;
    new_value: any;
  }[];
  error_message?: string; // Captured validation or conflict descriptions
  timestamp: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
}
```

### 9.5 Academic Year Snapshot & Archiving
At the completion of every academic year, constitutional operational metadata shall be snapshotted in its entirety. This captures a read-only historical record of student enrollments, teaching allocations, and governance assignments. This immutable snapshot is used to support historical reporting, professional evaluation (PBPPP) tracking, quality audit (SKPMg2) compliance, long-term system audits, and longitudinal analytics.

---

## 10. Conclusion

By establishing **Layer 0 - School Configuration Layer** and **Layer 3 - Teaching Resource Allocation** as core constitutional guidelines, the **iSEPADU AI** platform achieves perfect structural flexibility. SJK(C) Ek Hua and any future adopting school can dynamically configure their governance models, curriculum subjects, class names, and teacher workloads entirely through metadata. This configuration-first framework guarantees long-term stability, lightweight resource utilization, and uncompromised compliance with national school standard operating procedures.

# iSEF Engine 00: School Governance Engine
## Constitutional & Organizational Specification v1.0

## 1. Executive Summary & Core Philosophy

The School Governance Engine (**Engine 00**) is the foundational, constitutional layer of the **iSEPADU AI** platform. It defines the formal organizational structures, governance portfolios, accountability matrices, and delegation boundaries of SJK(C) Ek Hua. 

No project, activity, financial action, or administrative decision exists in isolation. Under the Integrated School Execution Framework (iSEF), every record, entity, and workflow must inherit its governance context from **Engine 00**. This ensures that all activities are aligned with official Ministry of Education (KPM) mandates, local school charter standards, and the Malaysia School Governance (**MySG**) framework.

---

## 2. School Organizational & Command Structure

The organizational topology of SJK(C) Ek Hua follows a highly disciplined, multi-tier command structure. This hierarchy translates policy from national executive directives into operational excellence in the classroom.

```
                  ┌────────────────────────────────────────┐
                  │          GURU BESAR (Principal)        │
                  │        [Apex Executive Authority]      │
                  └───────────────────┬────────────────────┘
                                      │
         ┌────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┐
         ▼                            ▼                            ▼
┌──────────────────┐         ┌──────────────────┐         ┌──────────────────┐
│  PK PENTADBIRAN  │         │      PK HEM      │         │  PK KOKURIKULUM  │
│ [Academic Lead]  │         │ [Student Affairs]│         │ [Co-Curric Lead] │
└────────┬─────────┘         └────────┬─────────┘         └────────┬─────────┘
         │                            │                            │
   ┌─────┴─────┐                ┌─────┴─────┐                ┌─────┴─────┐
   ▼           ▼                ▼           ▼                ▼           ▼
┌───────┐ ┌─────────┐        ┌───────┐ ┌─────────┐        ┌───────┐ ┌─────────┐
│Ketua  │ │Guru     │        │Penyel │ │Guru     │        │Penyel │ │Guru     │
│Panitia│ │Subjek   │        │Disipl │ │Kelas    │        │Sukan/ │ │Penasihat│
└───────┘ └─────────┘        └───────┘ └─────────┘        └───────┘ └─────────┘
```

### 2.1 The Four Spheres of Organizational Authority
1.  **Apex Executive Level (Guru Besar):**
    *   Holds ultimate legal, financial, and pedagogical responsibility for the school.
    *   Formulates school strategy, authorizes macro-resource allocation, and serves as the final arbiter for school policies.
2.  **Portfolio Executive Level (Penolong Kanan - PK):**
    *   **PK Pentadbiran (PK 1):** Academic curriculum management, teaching rosters, scheduling, and staff development.
    *   **PK Hal Ehwal Murid (PK HEM):** Student discipline, safety, welfare, health, guidance counseling, and enrollment.
    *   **PK Kokurikulum (PK Koko):** Co-curricular activities, sports houses, uniform bodies, clubs, and external competitions.
3.  **Middle Management & Coordination Level (Ketua Panitia / Penyelaras):**
    *   **Ketua Panitia (Heads of Department):** Subject-specific curriculum leaders. Responsible for subject performance, teacher training, lesson plan alignments, and learning resource management.
    *   **Penyelaras (Coordinators):** Dedicated leaders managing cross-functional programs (e.g., Penyelaras SPBT for textbooks, Penyelaras Disiplin for discipline).
4.  **Operational Execution Level (Guru & AKP):**
    *   **Guru (Teachers):** Direct implementation of curriculum, classroom pedagogy, continuous assessment (PBD), and student mentoring. Includes Subject Teachers and Class Teachers.
    *   **Anggota Kumpulan Pelaksana (AKP - Administrative/Support Staff):** Financial clerks, office administrators, and laboratory/ICT technicians supporting core operations.

---

## 3. MySG Portfolios & Jawatankuasa (Committees)

The Malaysian School Governance (**MySG**) model establishes standard operating committees. Engine 00 codifies these committees into persistent functional nodes.

### 3.1 Jawatankuasa Pengurusan & Pentadbiran Sekolah (JKPPS)
*   **Purpose:** The central cabinet governing daily school operations, strategic planning, finance, human resources, and crisis response.
*   **Composition:**
    *   *Pengerusi (Chair):* Guru Besar
    *   *Naib Pengerusi (Vice Chair):* PK Pentadbiran, PK HEM, PK Kokurikulum
    *   *Setiausaha (Secretary):* Appointed Senior Teacher
*   **Accountability:** School financial audits, physical infrastructure readiness, school-wide Key Performance Indicators (KPIs).

### 3.2 Jawatankuasa Kurikulum Sekolah (JKKS)
*   **Purpose:** The central academic governing body ensuring standard pedagogy, continuous classroom assessment (PBD), and public examinations performance.
*   **Composition:**
    *   *Pengerusi:* Guru Besar
    *   *Timbalan Pengerusi:* PK Pentadbiran
    *   *Setiausaha:* Setiausaha Peperiksaan / Kurikulum
    *   *Ahli Jawatankuasa (Members):* All Ketua Panitia (Language, Math, Science, Humanities, etc.)
*   **Accountability:** Class-wide TP (Tahap Penguasaan) distributions, academic intervention design, exam analytical reports.

### 3.3 Jawatankuasa Hal Ehwal Murid (JKHEM)
*   **Purpose:** Governing student welfare, safety, counseling, health (RMT), textbook loan schemes (SPBT), and school discipline.
*   **Composition:**
    *   *Pengerusi:* Guru Besar
    *   *Timbalan Pengerusi:* PK HEM
    *   *Setiausaha:* Setiausaha HEM
    *   *Ahli Jawatankuasa:* Penyelaras Disiplin, Guru Bimbingan & Kaunseling, Penyelaras SPBT, Penyelaras Kantin.
*   **Accountability:** Student attendance rates, disciplinary case logs, health and safety compliance, welfare disbursement audits.

### 3.4 Jawatankuasa Kokurikulum Sekolah (JKKOKO)
*   **Purpose:** Overseeing student engagement in co-curricular areas (sports, uniform units, associations) and preparing students for regional and national tournaments.
*   **Composition:**
    *   *Pengerusi:* Guru Besar
    *   *Timbalan Pengerusi:* PK Kokurikulum
    *   *Setiausaha:* Setiausaha Kokurikulum
    *   *Ahli Jawatankuasa:* Penyelaras Unit Beruniform, Penyelaras Persatuan/Kelab, Penyelaras Sukan/Permainan.
*   **Accountability:** Student participation percentages (PAJSK), co-curricular point systems, athletic event management, safety during physical activities.

---

## 4. Authority & RACI Matrix

Decisions, approvals, and actions executed within iSEPADU AI are strictly governed by this constitutional RACI Matrix.

| Operational Decision or Workflow Item | Guru Besar (GB) | Penolong Kanan (PK) | Ketua Panitia / Penyelaras | Guru Subjek / Kelas | AKP |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Formulating Strategic School Plan (Takwim)** | **A** | **R** | **C** | **C** | **I** |
| **Approving Academic Intervention Project (iSEF)** | **A** | **R** | **C** | **I** | **I** |
| **Executing Subject-Level Intervention Actions** | **I** | **A** | **C** | **R** | **I** |
| **Continuous Assessment (PBD) Data Sign-Off** | **A** | **R** | **R** | **R** | **I** |
| **Financial Budget/Disbursement Approval** | **A** | **R** | **C** | **I** | **R** |
| **Student Disciplinary Suspension / Action** | **A** | **R** | **C** | **I** | **I** |
| **Co-Curricular Tournament Registration** | **A** | **R** | **R** | **R** | **C** |

*   **R (Responsible):** The role directly tasked with doing the work or executing the step.
*   **A (Accountable):** The role with final signing/approval authority. Only one role can be Accountable per decision node.
*   **C (Consulted):** Key stakeholders who provide input, expertise, or feedback prior to the decision/action.
*   **I (Informed):** Roles notified upon decision finalization or action completion.

---

## 5. Escalation & Delegation Protocols

To ensure organizational continuity, Engine 00 mandates the following escalation and delegation mechanisms:

### 5.1 Delegation of Signing Authority (Acting Roles)
*   **Rule of Succession:** In the physical absence of the Guru Besar, signing authority and system executive permissions automatically cascade to:
    1.  *First Successor:* PK Pentadbiran
    2.  *Second Successor:* PK HEM
    3.  *Third Successor:* PK Kokurikulum
*   **System Action:** When delegation is active, acting personnel retain system-wide auditing capability, with actions logged under the delegated account with the tag `[DELEGATED_AUTHORITY]`.

### 5.2 Disciplinary & Compliance Escalation Pathway
When a checkpoint or milestone within the school's operational framework fails to meet deadlines or compliance targets, the issue is systematically escalated:
1.  **Stage 1 (Internal Revision):** Action returned to the assigned PIC (Teacher) with constructive review notes. (Buffer: 3 Days).
2.  **Stage 2 (Departmental Escalation):** If resolved actions are still pending after 5 days, notifications escalate to the Ketua Panitia or Penyelaras. Co-signing or peer-mentorship is initiated.
3.  **Stage 3 (Executive Intervention):** If outstanding past 10 days, notifications escalate directly to the respective Penolong Kanan (PK) for formal administrative coaching.
4.  **Stage 4 (Strategic Review):** Beyond 14 days, the issue is placed on the agenda of JKPPS, presided by the Guru Besar, for institutional remediation.

---

## 6. Integration Architecture (Constitutional Context Inheritance)

Engine 00 acts as the ultimate reference database. Every record generated in downstream engines and modules inherits properties from the Governance Engine:

```
                      ┌─────────────────────────────────┐
                      │    ENGINE 00: GOVERNANCE        │
                      │ (Committees, Roles, Portfolios) │
                      └────────────────┬────────────────┘
                                       │
                                       │ Inherits Organizational Identity
                                       ▼
        ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
        │                        ENGINE 01 (iSEF)                      │
        │                                                              │
        │  • Project Owner $\in$ Jawatankuasa (e.g., JKKS)            │
        │  • Approving Admin $\in$ Portfolio Executives (e.g., PK 1)  │
        │  • PIC Assignment $\in$ School Organization Directory       │
        └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

When a teacher starts an intervention project or logs an activity inside a workspace (e.g., Academic or HEM), the platform resolves:
1.  **Who is creating this?** (Resolves UID $\to$ Role in SJKC Ek Hua Directory).
2.  **Under which committee does this fall?** (Resolves Academic $\to$ Jawatankuasa Kurikulum Sekolah - JKKS).
3.  **Who has approval authority over this project?** (Resolves Jawatankuasa Chair/Vice-chair $\to$ PK Pentadbiran or Guru Besar).
4.  **What compliance standards apply?** (Inherits standard KPM and school-defined execution policies).

---

## 7. Future Strategic Framework Alignments

To ensure that SJK(C) Ek Hua's governance structures translate directly into national metrics, Engine 00 is designed to map onto standard Malaysian educational assessment frameworks:

### 7.1 PBPPP Alignment (Penilaian Bersepadu Pegawai Perkhidmatan Pendidikan)
*   **Dimension Mapping:** Active participation in iSEF intervention projects directly feeds into a teacher's annual performance appraisal (PBPPP) across key criteria:
    *   *Profesionalisme:* Timely completion of actions, administrative accountability, and initiative.
    *   *Penglibatan:* Collaborative role in committees and inter-departmental cooperation.
    *   *Keberhasilan:* Dynamic classroom assessment outcomes (PBD TP improvements) derived from execution results.

### 7.2 SKPMg2 Alignment (Standard Kualiti Pendidikan Malaysia Gelombang 2)
Engine 00 logs institutional compliance data directly matching the four core operational standards of SKPMg2:
*   **Standard 1: Kepimpinan (Leadership):** Captures how the Guru Besar guides staff performance, monitors goals, and delegates resources.
*   **Standard 2: Pengurusan Organisasi (Organizational Management):** Monitors the efficiency of committees, assets, financial management, and school infrastructure.
*   **Standard 3: Pengurusan Kurikulum, Kokurikulum & HEM (Student Program Management):** Aggregates data from active projects across the respective workspaces to determine program effectiveness.
*   **Standard 4: Pembelajaran & Pemudahcaraan (PdPc - Classroom Pedagogy):** Correlates teacher execution performance with active intervention progress and classroom results.

### 7.3 School Health Index
*   **Definition:** A unified operational health score for SJK(C) Ek Hua compiled continuously across portfolios.
*   **Components:**
    *   *Academic Health:* Baseline class performance (PBD averages), intervention completion velocity, and exam pass rates.
    *   *Welfare & Social Health:* Disciplinary case rates, student attendance, counseling engagement, and dietary program coverage.
    *   *Co-curricular Health:* PAJSK participation rates, tournament registrations, and team placement points.
    *   *Governance Health:* Committee meeting compliance, budget audit scores, and action-item delay statistics.

---

## 8. Conclusion

By establishing **Engine 00: School Governance Engine** as the constitutional bedrock, iSEPADU AI ensures that every operational activity, assessment record, and strategic project is aligned with the organizational reality of SJK(C) Ek Hua. This constitutional layout provides the necessary structures for seamless future development of all administrative, academic, welfare, and co-curricular sub-systems.

# iSEPADU AI — Platform Layer Architecture Specification
## Platform Freeze v1.1 (Final) — Architecture Decision Notice

This document establishes the permanent, reusable **Platform Layer** of iSEPADU AI. All future modules, business engines, and external workspaces (e.g., Kurikulum, HEM, Kokurikulum, Pentadbiran) must consume these centralized services. Direct database mutations, raw external API integration, or duplicated business logic are strictly prohibited.

---

## 1. Platform Layer Conceptual Diagram

```mermaid
graph TD
    %% Business & Application Layer
    subgraph Business_Engines [Business & Application Layer]
        E00[Engine 00: School Governance]
        E01[Engine 01: Project Lifecycle]
        M01[HEM Workspace]
        M02[Kokurikulum Workspace]
        M03[Kurikulum Workspace]
    end

    %% Reusable Platform Services Layer (Middleware)
    subgraph Platform_Layer [Unified Reusable Platform Layer]
        S01[1. Identity Service]
        S02[2. Governance Service]
        S03[3. Audit Service]
        S04[4. Validation Service]
        S05[5. IDS Service]
        S06[6. Evidence Service]
        S07[7. Notification Service]
        S08[8. Scheduling Service]
        S09[9. Reporting Service]
        S10[10. Academic Transition Service]
        S11[11. Approval Service]
        S12[12. AI Gateway Service]
    end

    %% Foundation Layer
    subgraph Foundation_Layer [Data & Storage Layer - SSOT]
        DB[(Firestore Database)]
        ST[Cloud Storage Bucket]
        AUTH[Firebase Auth]
    end

    %% Connections
    Business_Engines --> S01
    Business_Engines --> S02
    Business_Engines --> S04
    Business_Engines --> S05
    Business_Engines --> S11
    Business_Engines --> S12

    Platform_Layer --> DB
    Platform_Layer --> ST
    Platform_Layer --> AUTH
```

## 2. Core Platform Services Specifications

### 2.1 Identity Service

#### Purpose
Coordinates centralized authentication, demographic mapping, and permission resolution for all system actors (staff, students, parents, external contributors).

#### Responsibilities
*   **User Authentication Integration:** Wrap Firebase Auth tokens.
*   **Profile Unification:** Seamlessly map a single authentication record to personnel data (IC, Grade, Status) or student data (MyKid, Enrollment).
*   **Session Resolution:** Detect current sessional parameters (active academic year, current active dashboard).
*   **RBAC (Role-Based Access Control):** Enforce hierarchical roles (Administrator, Pengetua/Guru Besar, PK, Guru, PPM, Penjaga, Pelawat).

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export interface UserIdentity {
  uid: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'ADMIN' | 'GB' | 'PK' | 'TEACHER' | 'PPM' | 'PARENT' | 'EXTERNAL';
  meta: {
    personnelId?: string;
    studentIds?: string[];
    gradeCode?: string;
    isActive: boolean;
  };
}

export class IdentityService {
  /** Resolves current logged-in session context */
  static async getCurrentSession(): Promise<{
    user: UserIdentity;
    activeAcademicYear: string;
  }>;

  /** Validates user claims against designated administrative permissions */
  static async checkPermission(uid: string, permission: string): Promise<boolean>;

  /** Resolves demographic profiles for students */
  static async getStudentDemographics(studentId: string): Promise<any>;
}
```

#### Event Handlers & Lifecycle
*   **Published Events:**
    *   `identity.login.success` -> Emitted when a user authenticates.
    *   `identity.role.escalated` -> Emitted when credentials are temporarily elevated.
*   **Consumed Events:**
    *   `academic.transition.completed` -> Triggers refresh of default sessional caches.

#### Dependencies
*   Firebase Authentication SDK
*   `StudentProfile` & `PersonnelMaster` collections

#### Security Boundaries
*   Only system administrators may edit roles or link external IDs.
*   No financial or welfare indicators can be mapped to client-side identity objects.

---

### 2.2 Governance Service

#### Purpose
Directs organization structure, authority resolution, portfolio assignments, and RACI matrices based on the Malaysia School Governance (MySG) standards.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Organizational Hierarchy:** Maintain linear and committee reporting layouts.
*   **Portfolio Mapping:** Track active portfolios (Sektor Kurikulum, HEM, Kokurikulum, PPKI).
*   **RACI Matrix Enforcement:** Evaluate who is Responsible (R), Accountable (A), Consulted (C), or Informed (I) for any process.
*   **Committee Management:** Resolve temporary task forces, standard committees, and coordinators.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export interface GovernanceAssignment {
  id: string;
  personnelId: string;
  academicYear: string;
  governanceType: 'PORTFOLIO' | 'COMMITTEE' | 'COORDINATOR' | 'ADDITIONAL_RESPONSIBILITY';
  entityName: string; // e.g. "Jawatankuasa SPBT"
  role: 'CHAIR' | 'SECRETARY' | 'COMMITTEE_MEMBER' | 'COORDINATOR_LEAD' | 'OPERATIONAL_STAFF';
  raciRole: 'R' | 'A' | 'C' | 'I';
}

export class GovernanceService {
  /** Resolves the designated Accountable (A) personnel for an administrative entity */
  static async resolveAccountableParty(entityName: string, academicYear: string): Promise<string[]>;

  /** Verifies if a user has approval eligibility for a given decision slot */
  static async evaluateRaciAccess(
    userId: string, 
    entityName: string, 
    action: 'APPROVE' | 'EDIT' | 'EXECUTE'
  ): Promise<boolean>;

  /** Traverses organizational trees to compile hierarchical escalation paths */
  static async getEscalationChain(entityName: string): Promise<string[]>;
}
```

#### Event Handlers & Lifecycle
*   **Published Events:**
    *   `governance.assignment.created` -> Emitted when an administrative role is delegated.
    *   `governance.expiry.reached` -> Automatic trigger when a temporary task force crosses its deadline.
*   **Consumed Events:**
    *   `personnel.terminated` -> Revokes active governance roles automatically.

#### Dependencies
*   `governance_assignments` collection
*   `IdentityService`

---

### 2.3 Audit Service

#### Purpose
Maintains a tamper-evident, transactional, and append-only database of every change, override, bypass, and rollback.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Audit Logging:** Append structured, irreversible logs for every database write.
*   **Override Logs:** Log all administrative overrides with mandatory human justifications.
*   **Rollback Auditing:** Maintain an exact cell-by-cell state mapping to enable clean reversals.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export interface AuditTrailEntry {
  id?: string;
  timestamp: string;
  actor_id: string;
  actor_name: string;
  actor_role: string;
  action_type: 'CREATE' | 'UPDATE' | 'DELETE' | 'STATE_CHANGE' | 'AUTHORIZE' | 'IMPORT' | 'ROLLBACK';
  layer: number;
  entity_id: string;
  details: string;
}

export class AuditService {
  /** Appends an immutable record to the global audit logs */
  static async log(entry: Omit<AuditTrailEntry, 'id' | 'timestamp'>): Promise<string>;

  /** Retrieves historical transaction logs filterable by context */
  static async fetchLogs(filters: {
    layer?: number;
    action_type?: string;
    actor_id?: string;
  }): Promise<AuditTrailEntry[]>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `audit_trail` Firestore collection

#### Security Boundaries
*   Strictly append-only: Deletions, edits, or overwrites on the `audit_trail` collection are forbidden at the database levels via security rules.

---

### 2.4 Validation Service

#### Purpose
Asserts structural data integrity, constitutional rules, and policy configurations before records are committed to the SSOT.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Workload Checks (Rule T1):** Assert that a teacher does not exceed standard period thresholds (32 periods/week) unless authorized.
*   **Solitary Main Teacher (Rule T2):** Assert that no class-subject has multiple MAIN teachers assigned concurrently.
*   **Sessional Separation:** Enforce year-specific isolation checks.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export interface ValidationResponse {
  isPassed: boolean;
  errorCode?: string;
  message: string;
  requiresOverride: boolean;
}

export class ValidationService {
  /** Performs comprehensive pre-commit checks on a proposed subject allocation */
  static async validateSubjectAllocation(
    teacherId: string, 
    classId: string, 
    subjectName: string, 
    periods: number,
    academicYear: string
  ): Promise<ValidationResponse>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `subject_allocations` & `classes` collections

---

### 2.5 IDS (Intelligent Import) Service

#### Purpose
Governs bulk data parsing, cell-level smart matching, and safe rollbacks.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Roster Analysis:** Parse raw JSON or CSV; differentiate rows into `INSERT`, `FILL_EMPTY`, `CONFLICT`, or `SKIP`.
*   **Snapshotting:** Log previous document states under sessional ids before executing changes.
*   **Rollback Coordination:** Provide clean, non-disruptive rollbacks.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export class IdsService {
  /** Parses and matches raw roster text against current database profiles */
  static analyzeRoster(raw: string, type: 'student' | 'personnel'): Promise<any>;

  /** Commits verified records and logs exact pre-import snapshots for rollback */
  static async commitSession(preview: any[], type: 'student' | 'personnel', actor: string): Promise<string>;

  /** Rollbacks a targeted session using historical snapshots */
  static async rollbackSession(sessionId: string, actor: string): Promise<boolean>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `rollback_records` & `import_sessions` collections
*   `AuditService`

---

### 2.6 Evidence Service

#### Purpose
Provides storage abstractions, verification steps, and metadata registries for evidentiary files attached to execution records.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Metadata Registration:** Store descriptive tags, creator IDs, and sizes.
*   **Verification:** Support digital sign-off of documents by administrative reviewers.
*   **Provider Abstraction:** Abstract physical storages (local storage, Google Drive integration, or remote files).

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export interface EvidenceMetadata {
  id: string;
  sourceType: 'IMAGE' | 'PDF' | 'VIDEO' | 'GOOGLE_DRIVE' | 'URL';
  uri: string;
  uploadedBy: string;
  timestamp: string;
  isVerified: boolean;
  verifiedBy?: string;
}

export class EvidenceService {
  /** Registers a file/URL as active evidence under a project action plan */
  static async registerEvidence(evidence: Omit<EvidenceMetadata, 'id' | 'timestamp' | 'isVerified'>): Promise<string>;

  /** Marks an evidence file as officially verified by an authorized PK/Head */
  static async verifyEvidence(evidenceId: string, verifierId: string): Promise<boolean>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `evidence_metadata` collection
*   `IdentityService`

---

### 2.7 Notification Service

#### Purpose
Funnels and delivers alerts, progress prompts, and meeting reminders through internal feeds and external gateways (Email, WhatsApp).

#### Responsibilities
*   **Triggered Reminders:** Execute periodic notification cron-jobs (7 days, 3 days, 1 day, same day, 5 minutes before).
*   **Broadcasts:** Publish school-wide, rich notices onto the "Shared Notice Board".
*   **Escalations:** Trigger notifications automatically to higher authority nodes when actions fall overdue.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export class NotificationService {
  /** Directs an alert to a user's feed */
  static async notifyUser(userId: string, title: string, body: string, priority: 'LOW' | 'HIGH'): Promise<void>;

  /** Dispatches an urgent external notification */
  static async dispatchExternal(
    recipientContact: string, 
    channel: 'WHATSAPP' | 'EMAIL', 
    templateId: string, 
    variables: Record<string, string>
  ): Promise<boolean>;
}
```

#### Consumed Events
*   `action.overdue` -> Automatically triggers notification escalations.

---

### 2.8 Scheduling Service

#### Purpose
Directs timetables, academic calendars, bookings, and meetings to eliminate collision risks.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Timetable Resolution:** Check and assert slot-level availability of classrooms and teachers.
*   **Duty Rosters:** Manage weekly administrative monitoring assignments.
*   **Resource Bookings:** Lock physical rooms or equipment dynamically.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export class SchedulingService {
  /** Evaluates if a given time slot has room or personnel collisions */
  static async checkCollisions(
    timeSlot: string, 
    resourceId: string, 
    resourceType: 'TEACHER' | 'ROOM'
  ): Promise<boolean>;

  /** Schedules a virtual, physical, or academic meeting */
  static async createScheduleEvent(eventPayload: any): Promise<string>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `academic_calendar` & `timetables` collections

---

### 2.9 Reporting Service

#### Purpose
Compiles and generates formatted exports, evaluations, and compliance reports.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Quality Metrics Compilation:** Read historical records and evaluate scores against standard frameworks (SKPMg2, PBPPP).
*   **Co-curricular Tracking:** Calculate sessional points and export school statistics (PAJSK, PBD).
*   **Export Pipeline:** Generate formatted excel/CSV tables for administrative workloads.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export class ReportingService {
  /** Compiles a complete SKPMg2 digital evaluation file */
  static async compileSkpmEvaluation(academicYear: string): Promise<any>;

  /** Prepares an PAJSK/PBD performance digest */
  static async generateStudentDigest(studentId: string, academicYear: string): Promise<any>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   All active sessional databases

---

### 2.10 Academic Transition Service

#### Purpose
Safeguards end-of-year workflows, promotions, snaps, transfers, and graduations.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Sessional Promotion:** Batch-create new student-enrollment links for new academic terms.
*   **Yearly Snapshots:** Convert previous sessional files to immutable, read-only structures (Rule M2).
*   **Student Graduation:** Mark cohorts as graduated; archive demographics cleanly.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export class AcademicTransitionService {
  /** Commits an immutable snapshot of sessional configurations and allocations */
  static async executeYearlySnapshot(academicYear: string): Promise<boolean>;

  /** Batch promotes filtered cohorts into succeeding classes */
  static async promoteCohort(
    studentIds: string[], 
    sourceClassId: string, 
    targetClassId: string, 
    targetYear: string
  ): Promise<boolean>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `AuditService`
*   `IdentityService`

### 2.11 Approval Service

#### Purpose
Brokers multi-level decision workflows, acting delegations, and digital signature sign-offs.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Multi-level Routing:** Ensure action plans are checked by coordinators, validated by PK, and approved by GB.
*   **Acting Officer Assignments:** Redirect delegation credentials dynamically when an officer is on leave.
*   **Sign-offs:** Validate cryptographic-style approval timestamps.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export class ApprovalService {
  /** Routes an approval ticket to the next authorized node */
  static async submitForApproval(ticketId: string, workflowType: string): Promise<string>;

  /** Sets up an acting delegation assignment for temporary portfolio coverage */
  static async setDelegation(fromUserId: string, toUserId: string, expiry: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   `GovernanceService`

### 2.12 AI Gateway Service

#### Purpose
Serves as the unified server-side entry point for all cognitive tasks.

#### Responsibilities
*   **Provider Abstraction:** Translate unified commands into target provider payloads (Gemini SDK, OpenAI API, local LLMs).
*   **Token Protection:** Hide secret API keys inside server configurations (No direct client calls).
*   **Caching & Optimization:** Cache general text summarizations to minimize API overhead.

#### Public Interface
```typescript
export interface AiPromptPayload {
  prompt: string;
  contextData?: any;
  temperature?: number;
}

export class AiGatewayService {
  /** Unified server-side entry point for generating textual analyses */
  static async generateText(payload: AiPromptPayload): Promise<string>;

  /** Performs multi-modal analyses on uploaded evidence or student results */
  static async analyzeDocument(fileUri: string, schemaRequirement: string): Promise<any>;
}
```

#### Dependencies
*   Server-side Gemini SDK (`@google/genai`) and secure credentials.

---

## 3. Platform Architecture Policies

### 3.1 Error & Recovery Strategy
1.  **Fail-Safe Defaults:** On database connection errors, all validation checks default to `REJECT` (safe configuration mode).
2.  **State-Reversion:** Failed batch transactions must roll back intermediate Firestore updates completely.
3.  **Fallback Audits:** When external providers (e.g., WhatsApp Gateway or AI API) throw errors, logging actions must still proceed to notify administrators of delivery failures.

### 3.2 Security Boundaries
*   **Layer 1 (Direct Storage Rules):** Prevent direct browser deletions or mutations of audit trails, historical sessions, and snapshotted structures.
*   **Context Verification:** Every read or write query evaluates the operator's dynamic governance parameters to deny access to portfolios they do not manage.

### 3.3 Cross-Service Communication Rules
*   **Event-Driven Communication:** Services emit local, non-blocking TypeScript events for informational tasks (e.g., publishing a notice board alert).
*   **Synchronous Checks:** Crucial validation processes (such as period limit validations or RACI permissions checks) must complete synchronously before any Firestore updates are triggered.

---

## 4. Architectural Constitutional Rules (Platform Freeze v1.1)

To ensure long-term platform stability, maintain absolute decoupling, and enforce absolute reuse, the iSEPADU AI framework defines the following immutable structural rules.

### Rule W1 — Workflow Uniformity
Every business engine in iSEPADU AI shall share one identical execution lifecycle.

$$\text{Plan} \longrightarrow \text{Approve} \longrightarrow \text{Execute} \longrightarrow \text{Evidence} \longrightarrow \text{Monitoring} \longrightarrow \text{Evaluation} \longrightarrow \text{Archive}$$

No workspace or administrative module is permitted to redefine this workflow. Academic, HEM, Kokurikulum, Administration, PPKI, and Prasekolah must all consume this lifecycle through **Engine 01 (Project Lifecycle Engine)**. Only module-specific business rules, validation structures, and data schemas may differ.

### Rule W2 — Engine Ownership
Every business engine owns strictly and exclusively its own domain logic:
*   **Academic Engine:** Standard School Assessments (PBD, UASA), Student Interventions, Academic Performance Analytics.
*   **HEM (Hal Ehwal Murid) Engine:** Daily Student Attendance, Student Discipline Records, Welfare and Financial Assistance Systems.
*   **Kokurikulum Engine:** Sports & Co-curricular Scoring (PAJSK), Activity Records, Regional and National Competitions.
*   **PPKI (Program Pendidikan Khas Integrasi) Engine:** Special Education Adaptive Tracking, Individual Learning Plans (ILP / RPI).
*   **Prasekolah Engine:** National Preschool Standard Curriculum (KSPK) tracking, Portfolio Assessments, Developmental Checklists.

Business engines are strictly prohibited from implementing or duplicating their own project/activity lifecycle management. All progress tracking, approval routing, and task monitoring must be delegated to Engine 01.

### Rule W3 — Layer Dependency Rule
The iSEPADU AI platform enforces a strict unidirectional dependency graph:

$$\text{Workspace (UI/Feature Module)} \longrightarrow \text{Business Engine} \longrightarrow \text{Platform Services} \longrightarrow \text{Infrastructure}$$

*   Upper layers may consume lower layers.
*   Lower layers must never depend on, import, or hold references to upper layers.
*   Direct Workspace-to-Workspace communication is strictly prohibited. All cross-workspace communication is channeled via underlying Platform Services or explicit data aggregation layers.

### Rule W4 — Cross-Workspace Isolation
Business workspaces must remain logically and architecturally decoupled to prevent tight integration rot:
*   The **Academic Workspace** must not directly import or query HEM database schemas.
*   The **HEM Workspace** must not directly query Kokurikulum database schemas.
*   The **Kokurikulum Workspace** must not directly query Administration schemas.
*   Cross-domain information shall exclusively be exchanged through approved, decoupled platform services (e.g., Reporting Service) or future specialized aggregation engines.

### Rule W5 — Workflow Templates
To facilitate rapid annual school setup, Engine 01 projects must support complete reusability templates. A finished project or task-force sequence may be duplicated into a new academic session, preserving:
*   Activities & Milestone Structures
*   Action & Sub-action Sequences
*   Reminder Policies & Frequency Configurations
*   Approval Delegation & Governance Hierarchies

*Constraint:* The duplicated project must start with a completely clean execution history. Under no circumstances shall historical evidence uploads, monitoring comments, log notes, or audit history be copied into the new instance.

### Rule W6 — Event-Driven Communication
Business engines must achieve high cohesion and loose coupling by publishing asynchronous, decoupled domain events instead of executing direct RPCs or importing external engines.
*   **Key Emitted Events:**
    *   `ProjectCreated` — Fired when an administrator or PK creates a new operational plan.
    *   `ActionAssigned` — Fired when a sub-action is assigned to a specific personnel.
    *   `EvidenceSubmitted` — Fired when evidentiary documents or URLs are linked to an active action item.
    *   `MonitoringCompleted` — Fired when a designated monitor completes an evaluation note.
    *   `EvaluationCompleted` — Fired when the final project results are compiled.
*   **Consumers:** All platform services subscribe to these events independently. Future analytics services (e.g., School Health Index, Executive Decision Engines) will hook directly into this event bus without modifying existing codebase engines.

### Rule W7 — Constitutional Freeze
Following the ratification of Architecture Freeze v1.1, the core architectural structure of iSEPADU AI is officially frozen. No future sprint, module, or user request may redefine, alter, or bypass:
1.  The multi-layer Platform Services
2.  The Core Business Engines (Engine 00 & Engine 01)
3.  The Master Data Constitution (Layers 0 to 7)
4.  The Governance Hierarchy & RACI Structures
5.  The Uniform Workflow Constitution

All future efforts must focus on clean implementation, adding new business engines, external workspaces, and visual widgets without modifying this structural foundation.

---

### Additional Business Rule — Prasekolah Workspace Autonomy
The **Prasekolah (Preschool)** workspace shall remain a fully independent, isolated workspace.
*   **Workflow Execution:** It consumes the same uniform project lifecycle (Engine 01) for school activities, events, and task-forces.
*   **Curriculum & Assessment:** It maintains its own specialized assessments based on the Kurikulum Standard Prasekolah Kebangsaan (KSPK), portfolio collections, and child developmental milestones.
*   **Constraint:** No platform service or future module shall assume Preschool conforms to the standard primary academic assessment systems (Year 1 to Year 6) such as PBD, UASA, or standard grade scales.

---

## 5. Platform Rollout Consensus

This v1.1 specifications freeze serves as the official reusable platform layout. Future modules and workspaces of iSEPADU AI will build on top of this layer, directly utilizing these programmatic endpoints to guarantee compliance, single source of truth (SSOT) integrity, and strict architectural isolation.
